## 計算幾何欄位屬性規則 (資料管理工具) · 參數

**id**　cd62b136743057c2c1-6

**english**　Calculate Geometry Fields Attribute Rule

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 屬性規則工具集
- 模板

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/calculate-geometry-fields-attribute-rule.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/calculate-geometry-fields-attribute-rule.html

**parameter**　geometry_attributes

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 幾何屬性
- 將在其中計算指定幾何屬性的欄位。 您可以選擇現有欄位或提供新欄位名稱。 如果提供了新欄位名稱，則欄位類型將根據寫入該欄位的值類型確定。 計數屬性將寫入長整型欄位；面積、長度、x、y、z 座標以及 m 值屬性將寫入雙精度欄位；度分秒或 MGRS 等座標記法將寫入文本欄位。 除非另有說明，否則面積和長度屬性是使用 2D 笛卡爾算法的平面測量值。 值表列： Field (Existing or New)—Field in which to calculate a geometry property. Property—Geometry property to calculate into the target field. Area—An attribute will be added to store the area of each polygon feature. Area (geodesic)—An attribute will be added to store the shape-preserving geodesic area of each polygon feature. Centroid x-coordinate—An attribute will be added to store the centroid x-coordinate of each feature. Centroid y-coordinate—An attribute will be added to store the centroid y-coordinate of each feature. Centroid z-coordinate—An attribute will be added to store the centroid z-coordinate of each feature. Centroid m-value—An attribute will be added to store the centroid m-value of each feature. Central point x-coordinate—An attribute will be added to store the x-coordinate of a central point inside or on each feature. This point is the same as the centroid if the centroid is inside the feature; otherwise, it is an inner label point. Central point y-coordinate—An attribute will be added to store the y-coordinate of a central point inside or on each feature. This point is the same as the centroid if the centroid is inside the feature; otherwise, it is an inner label point. Central point z-coordinate—An attribute will be added to store the z-coordinate of a central point inside or on each feature. This point is the same as the centroid if the centroid is inside the feature; otherwise, it is an inner label point. Central point m-value—An attribute will be added to store the m-value of a central point inside or on each feature. This point is the same as the centroid if the centroid is inside the feature; otherwise, it is an inner label point. Number of curves—An attribute will be added to store the number of curves in each feature. Curves include elliptical arcs, circular arcs, and Bezier curves. Number of holes—An attribute will be added to store the number of interior holes within each polygon feature. Minimum x-coordinate—An attribute will be added to store the minimum x-coordinate of each feature's extent. Minimum y-coordinate—An attribute will be added to store the minimum y-coordinate of each feature's extent. Minimum z-coordinate—An attribute will be added to store the minimum z-coordinate of each feature's extent. Maximum x-coordinate—An attribute will be added to store the maximum x-coordinate of each feature's extent. Maximum y-coordinate—An attribute will be added to store the maximum y-coordinate of each feature's extent. Maximum z-coordinate—An attribute will be added to store the maximum z-coordinate of each feature's extent. Length—An attribute will be added to store the length of each line feature. Length (geodesic)—An attribute will be added to store the shape-preserving geodesic length of each line feature. Length (3D)—An attribute will be added to store the 3D length of each line feature. Line bearing—An attribute will be added to store the start-to-end bearing of each line feature. Values range from 0 to 360, with 0 meaning north, 90 east, 180 south, 270 west, and so on. Line angle—An attribute will be added to store the start-to-end arithmetic angle of each line feature. Values range from 0 to 360, with 0 meaning north, 90 east, 180 south, 270 west, and so on. Line start x-coordinate—An attribute will be added to store the x-coordinate of the start point of each line feature. Line start y-coordinate—An attribute will be added to store the y-coordinate of the start point of each line feature. Line start z-coordinate—An attribute will be added to store the z-coordinate of the start point of each line feature. Line start m-value—An attribute will be added to store the m-value of the start point of each line feature. Line end x-coordinate—An attribute will be added to store the x-coordinate of the end point of each line feature. Line end y-coordinate—An attribute will be added to store the y-coordinate of the end point of each line feature. Line end z-coordinate—An attribute will be added to store the z-coordinate of the end point of each line feature. Line end m-value—An attribute will be added to store the m-value of the end point of each line feature. Number of parts—An attribute will be added to store the number of parts comprising each feature. Number of vertices—An attribute will be added to store the number of points or vertices comprising each feature. Perimeter length—An attribute will be added to store the length of the perimeter or border of each polygon feature. Perimeter length (geodesic)—An attribute will be added to store the shape-preserving geodesic length of the perimeter or border of each polygon feature. Point x-coordinate—An attribute will be added to store the x-coordinate of each point feature. Point y-coordinate—An attribute will be added to store the y-coordinate of each point feature. Point z-coordinate—An attribute will be added to store the z-coordinate of each point feature. Point m-value—An attribute will be added to store the m-value of each point feature. Point x- and y-coordinate notation—An attribute will be added to store the x- and y-coordinate of each point feature formatted as a specified coordinate notation.
- Value Table

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/data-management/calculate-geometry-fields-attribute-rule.html#parameters

## 計算幾何欄位屬性規則 (資料管理工具) · 參數

**id**　cd62b136743057c2c1-10

**english**　Calculate Geometry Fields Attribute Rule

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 屬性規則工具集
- 模板

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/calculate-geometry-fields-attribute-rule.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/calculate-geometry-fields-attribute-rule.html

**parameter**　coordinate_format

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 座標格式 （可選）
- 將在計算座標、長度和面積時使用的座標系。 默認使用輸入圖徵的座標系。 與輸入相同—座標格式將為十進制度。 十進制度—座標格式將為結尾帶有主方向分量的度分秒 (DDD° MM' SSS.ss" &lt;N|S|E|W>)。 度分秒 (DDD° MM' SSS.ss" <N|S|E|W>)—座標格式將為開頭帶有主方向分量的度分秒 (&lt;N|S|E|W> DDD° MM' SSS.ss")。 度分秒 (<N|S|E|W> DDD° MM' SSS.ss")—座標格式將為開頭帶有正或負方向分量的度分秒 (&lt;+|-> DDD° MM' SSS.ss")。 度分秒 (<+|-> DDD° MM' SSS.ss")—座標格式將為開頭帶有正或負方向分量的打包為單個值的度分秒 (&lt;+|-> DDD.MMSSSss)。 度分秒 (<+|-> DDD.MMSSSss)—座標格式將為結尾帶有主方向分量的度十進制分 (DDD° MM.mmm' <N|S|E|W>)。 度十進制分 (DDD° MM.mmm' <N|S|E|W>)—座標格式將為開頭帶有主方向分量的度十進制分 (<N|S|E|W> DDD° MM.mmm')。 度十進制分 (<N|S|E|W> DDD° MM.mmm')—座標格式將為開頭帶有正或負方向分量的度十進制分 (<+|-> DDD° MM.mmm')。 度十進制分 (<+|-> DDD° MM.mmm')—座標格式將為全球區域參考系統。 全球區域參考系統以緯度和經度為基礎，將世界分為并細分為像元。
- String

## 計算幾何欄位屬性規則 (資料管理工具) · 派生輸出

**id**　cd62b136743057c2c1-15

**english**　Calculate Geometry Fields Attribute Rule

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 屬性規則工具集
- 模板

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/calculate-geometry-fields-attribute-rule.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/calculate-geometry-fields-attribute-rule.html

**parameter**　geometry_attributes

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- geometry_attributes [geometry_attributes,...]
- 將在其中計算指定幾何屬性的欄位。 您可以選擇現有欄位或提供新欄位名稱。 如果提供了新欄位名稱，則欄位類型將根據寫入該欄位的值類型確定。 計數屬性將寫入長整型欄位；面積、長度、x、y、z 座標以及 m 值屬性將寫入雙精度欄位；度分秒或 MGRS 等座標記法將寫入文本欄位。 除非另有說明，否則面積和長度屬性是使用 2D 笛卡爾算法的平面測量值。 值表列： Target Field—Field in which to calculate a geometry property. Property—Geometry property to calculate into the target field. AREA—An attribute will be added to store the area of each polygon feature. AREA_GEODESIC—An attribute will be added to store the shape-preserving geodesic area of each polygon feature. CENTROID_X—An attribute will be added to store the centroid x-coordinate of each feature. CENTROID_Y—An attribute will be added to store the centroid y-coordinate of each feature. CENTROID_Z—An attribute will be added to store the centroid z-coordinate of each feature. CENTROID_M—An attribute will be added to store the centroid m-value of each feature. INSIDE_X—An attribute will be added to store the x-coordinate of a central point inside or on each feature. This point is the same as the centroid if the centroid is inside the feature; otherwise, it is an inner label point. INSIDE_Y—An attribute will be added to store the y-coordinate of a central point inside or on each feature. This point is the same as the centroid if the centroid is inside the feature; otherwise, it is an inner label point. INSIDE_Z—An attribute will be added to store the z-coordinate of a central point inside or on each feature. This point is the same as the centroid if the centroid is inside the feature; otherwise, it is an inner label point. INSIDE_M—An attribute will be added to store the m-value of a central point inside or on each feature. This point is the same as the centroid if the centroid is inside the feature; otherwise, it is an inner label point. CURVE_COUNT—An attribute will be added to store the number of curves in each feature. Curves include elliptical arcs, circular arcs, and Bezier curves. HOLE_COUNT—An attribute will be added to store the number of interior holes within each polygon feature. EXTENT_MIN_X—An attribute will be added to store the minimum x-coordinate of each feature's extent. EXTENT_MIN_Y—An attribute will be added to store the minimum y-coordinate of each feature's extent. EXTENT_MIN_Z—An attribute will be added to store the minimum z-coordinate of each feature's extent. EXTENT_MAX_X—An attribute will be added to store the maximum x-coordinate of each feature's extent. EXTENT_MAX_Y—An attribute will be added to store the maximum y-coordinate of each feature's extent. EXTENT_MAX_Z—An attribute will be added to store the maximum z-coordinate of each feature's extent. LENGTH—An attribute will be added to store the length of each line feature. LENGTH_GEODESIC—An attribute will be added to store the shape-preserving geodesic length of each line feature. LENGTH_3D—An attribute will be added to store the 3D length of each line feature. LINE_BEARING—An attribute will be added to store the start-to-end bearing of each line feature. Values range from 0 to 360, with 0 meaning north, 90 east, 180 south, 270 west, and so on. LINE_ANGLE—An attribute will be added to store the start-to-end arithmetic angle of each line feature. Values range from 0 to 360, with 0 meaning north, 90 east, 180 south, 270 west, and so on. LINE_START_X—An attribute will be added to store the x-coordinate of the start point of each line feature. LINE_START_Y—An attribute will be added to store the y-coordinate of the start point of each line feature. LINE_START_Z—An attribute will be added to store the z-coordinate of the start point of each line feature. LINE_START_M—An attribute will be added to store the m-value of the start point of each line feature. LINE_END_X—An attribute will be added to store the x-coordinate of the end point of each line feature. LINE_END_Y—An attribute will be added to store the y-coordinate of the end point of each line feature. LINE_END_Z—An attribute will be added to store the z-coordinate of the end point of each line feature. LINE_END_M—An attribute will be added to store the m-value of the end point of each line feature. PART_COUNT—An attribute will be added to store the number of parts comprising each feature. POINT_COUNT—An attribute will be added to store the number of points or vertices comprising each feature. PERIMETER_LENGTH—An attribute will be added to store the length of the perimeter or border of each polygon feature. PERIMETER_LENGTH_GEODESIC—An attribute will be added to store the shape-preserving geodesic length of the perimeter or border of each polygon feature. POINT_X—An attribute will be added to store the x-coordinate of each point feature. POINT_Y—An attribute will be added to store the y-coordinate of each point feature. POINT_Z—An attribute will be added to store the z-coordinate of each point feature. POINT_M—An attribute will be added to store the m-value of each point feature. POINT_COORD_NOTATION—An attribute will be added to store the x- and y-coordinate of each point feature formatted as a specified coordinate notation.
- Value Table

**parameter**　coordinate_format

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- coordinate_format （可選）
- 將在計算座標、長度和面積時使用的座標系。 默認使用輸入圖徵的座標系。 SAME_AS_INPUT—座標格式將為十進制度。 DD—座標格式將為結尾帶有主方向分量的度分秒 (DDD° MM' SSS.ss" &lt;N|S|E|W>)。 DMS_DIR_LAST—座標格式將為開頭帶有主方向分量的度分秒 (&lt;N|S|E|W> DDD° MM' SSS.ss")。 DMS_DIR_FIRST—座標格式將為開頭帶有正或負方向分量的度分秒 (&lt;+|-> DDD° MM' SSS.ss")。 DMS_POS_NEG—座標格式將為開頭帶有正或負方向分量的打包為單個值的度分秒 (&lt;+|-> DDD.MMSSSss)。 DMS_PACKED—座標格式將為結尾帶有主方向分量的度十進制分 (DDD° MM.mmm' <N|S|E|W>)。 DDM_DIR_LAST—座標格式將為開頭帶有主方向分量的度十進制分 (<N|S|E|W> DDD° MM.mmm')。 DDM_DIR_FIRST—座標格式將為開頭帶有正或負方向分量的度十進制分 (<+|-> DDD° MM.mmm')。 DDM_POS_NEG—座標格式將為全球區域參考系統。 全球區域參考系統以緯度和經度為基礎，將世界分為并細分為像元。
- String

**id**　cd62b136743057c2c1-22

**text**　import arcpy
arcpy.env.workspace = r"C:\data\City.gdb"
arcpy.management.CalculateGeometryFieldsAttributeRule(
    in_table="test_class",
    out_folder=r"C:\temp",
    geometry_attributes="area_field AREA_GEODESIC",
    where_clause="OBJECTID = 1",
    length_unit="",
    area_unit="SQUARE_MILES_US",
    coordinate_format=""
)

## 計算幾何欄位屬性規則 (資料管理工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 屬性規則工具集
- 模板

**english**　Calculate Geometry Fields Attribute Rule

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/calculate-geometry-fields-attribute-rule.html

**id**　cd62b136743057c2c1-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/calculate-geometry-fields-attribute-rule.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Calculate Geometry Fields Attribute Rule，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入表 [in_table]、輸出文件夾 [out_folder]、幾何屬性 [geometry_attributes]、表達式 （可選） [where_clause]、長度單位 （可選） [length_unit]、面積單位 （可選） [area_unit]、座標格式 （可選） [coordinate_format]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**id**　cd62b136743057c2c1-schema

**kind**　版本參數表

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/calculate-geometry-fields-attribute-rule.html#parameters

**source_language**　en

**content_version**　3.8

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/data-management/calculate-geometry-fields-attribute-rule.html#parameters

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "in_table", "label_zh": "輸入表", "label_en": "Input Table", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Layer; Annotation Layer; Dimension Layer; Oriented Imagery Layer; Catalog Layer", "description_en": "The table or feature class that will have the new rule applied."}, {"parameter": "out_folder", "label_zh": "輸出文件夾", "label_en": "Output Folder", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Folder", "description_en": "The folder where the output will be saved."}, {"parameter": "geometry_attributes", "label_zh": "幾何屬性", "label_en": "Geometry Attributes", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Value Table", "description_en": "The fields in which the specified geometry properties will be calculated. You can select an existing field or provide a new field name. If a new field name is provided, the field type is determined by the type of values that are written to the field. Count attributes are written to long integer fields; area, length, and x-, y-, z-coordinate, and m-value attributes are written to double fields; and coordinate notations such as Degrees Minutes Seconds or MGRS are written to text fields. Unless otherwise noted, area and length properties are planar measurements using 2D Cartesian mathematics. Value table columns: Field (Existing or New)—Field in which to calculate a geometry property. Property—Geometry property to calculate into the target field. Area—An attribute will be added to store the area of each polygon feature. Area (geodesic)—An attribute will be added to store the shape-preserving geodesic area of each polygon feature. Centroid x-coordinate—An attribute will be added to store the centroid x-coordinate of each feature. Centroid y-coordinate—An attribute will be added to store the centroid y-coordinate of each feature. Centroid z-coordinate—An attribute will be added to store the centroid z-coordinate of each feature. Centroid m-value—An attribute will be added to store the centroid m-value of each feature. Central point x-coordinate—An attribute will be added to store the x-coordinate of a central point inside or on each feature. This point is the same as the centroid if the centroid is inside the feature; otherwise, it is an inner label point. Central point y-coordinate—An attribute will be added to store the y-coordinate of a central point inside or on each feature. This point is the same as the centroid if the centroid is inside the feature; otherwise, it is an inner label point. Central point z-coordinate—An attribute will be added to store the z-coordinate of a central point inside or on each feature. This point is the same as the centroid if the centroid is inside the feature; otherwise, it is an inner label point. Central point m-value—An attribute will be added to store the m-value of a central point inside or on each feature. This point is the same as the centroid if the centroid is inside the feature; otherwise, it is an inner label point. Number of curves—An attribute will be added to store the number of curves in each feature. Curves include elliptical arcs, circular arcs, and Bezier curves. Number of holes—An attribute will be added to store the number of interior holes within each polygon feature. Minimum x-coordinate—An attribute will be added to store the minimum x-coordinate of each feature's extent. Minimum y-coordinate—An attribute will be added to store the minimum y-coordinate of each feature's extent. Minimum z-coordinate—An attribute will be added to store the minimum z-coordinate of each feature's extent. Maximum x-coordinate—An attribute will be added to store the maximum x-coordinate of each feature's extent. Maximum y-coordinate—An attribute will be added to store the maximum y-coordinate of each feature's extent. Maximum z-coordinate—An attribute will be added to store the maximum z-coordinate of each feature's extent. Length—An attribute will be added to store the length of each line feature. Length (geodesic)—An attribute will be added to store the shape-preserving geodesic length of each line feature. Length (3D)—An attribute will be added to store the 3D length of each line feature. Line bearing—An attribute will be added to store the start-to-end bearing of each line feature. Values range from 0 to 360, with 0 meaning north, 90 east, 180 south, 270 west, and so on. Line angle—An attribute will be added to store the start-to-end arithmetic angle of each line feature. Values range from 0 to 360, with 0 meaning north, 90 east, 180 south, 270 west, and so on. Line start x-coordinate—An attribute will be added to store the x-coordinate of the start point of each line feature. Line start y-coordinate—An attribute will be added to store the y-coordinate of the start point of each line feature. Line start z-coordinate—An attribute will be added to store the z-coordinate of the start point of each line feature. Line start m-value—An attribute will be added to store the m-value of the start point of each line feature. Line end x-coordinate—An attribute will be added to store the x-coordinate of the end point of each line feature. Line end y-coordinate—An attribute will be added to store the y-coordinate of the end point of each line feature. Line end z-coordinate—An attribute will be added to store the z-coordinate of the end point of each line feature. Line end m-value—An attribute will be added to store the m-value of the end point of each line feature. Number of parts—An attribute will be added to store the number of parts comprising each feature. Number of vertices—An attribute will be added to store the number of points or vertices comprising each feature. Perimeter length—An attribute will be added to store the length of the perimeter or border of each polygon feature. Perimeter length (geodesic)—An attribute will be added to store the shape-preserving geodesic length of the perimeter or border of each polygon feature. Point x-coordinate—An attribute will be added to store the x-coordinate of each point feature. Point y-coordinate—An attribute will be added to store the y-coordinate of each point feature. Point z-coordinate—An attribute will be added to store the z-coordinate of each point feature. Point m-value—An attribute will be added to store the m-value of each point feature. Point x- and y-coordinate notation—An attribute will be added to store the x- and y-coordinate of each point feature formatted as a specified coordinate notation."}, {"parameter": "where_clause", "label_zh": "表達式 （可選）", "label_en": "Expression (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "SQL Expression", "description_en": "An SQL expression that will be used to limit the features from the input that will participate in the attribute rule."}, {"parameter": "length_unit", "label_zh": "長度單位 （可選）", "label_en": "Length Unit (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the unit that will be used to calculate length. Kilometers—The length unit will be kilometers. Meters—The length unit will be meters. Statute Miles—The length unit will be statute miles. International Nautical Miles—The length unit will be international nautical miles. International Yards—The length unit will be international yards. International Feet—The length unit will be international feet. US Survey Miles—The length unit will be US survey miles. US Survey Nautical Miles—The length unit will be US survey nautical miles. US Survey Yards—The length unit will be US survey yards. US Survey Feet—The length unit will be US survey feet."}, {"parameter": "area_unit", "label_zh": "面積單位 （可選）", "label_en": "Area Unit (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the unit that will be used to calculate area. Square Kilometers—The area unit will be square kilometers. Hectares—The area unit will be hectares. Square Meters—The area unit will be square meters. Square Statute Miles—The area unit will be square statute miles. Square International Nautical Miles—The area unit will be square international nautical miles. International Acres—The area unit will be international acres. Square International Yards—The area unit will be square international yards. Square International Feet—The area unit will be square international feet. Square US Survey Miles—The area unit will be square US survey miles. Square US Survey Nautical Miles—The area unit will be square US survey nautical miles. US Survey Acres—The area unit will be US survey acres. Square US Survey Yards—The area unit will be square US survey yards. Square US Survey Feet—The area unit will be square US survey feet."}, {"parameter": "coordinate_format", "label_zh": "座標格式 （可選）", "label_en": "Coordinate Format (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The coordinate system in which the coordinates, length, and area will be calculated. The coordinate system of the input features is used by default. Same as input—The coordinate format will be Decimal Degrees. Decimal Degrees—The coordinate format will be Degrees Minutes Seconds with cardinal direction component at the end (DDD° MM' SSS.ss\" <N|S|E|W>). Degrees Minutes Seconds (DDD° MM' SSS.ss\" <N|S|E|W>)—The coordinate format will be Degrees Minutes Seconds with cardinal direction component at the beginning (<N|S|E|W> DDD° MM' SSS.ss\"). Degrees Minutes Seconds (<N|S|E|W> DDD° MM' SSS.ss\")—The coordinate format will be Degrees Minutes Seconds with positive or negative direction component at the beginning (<+|-> DDD° MM' SSS.ss\"). Degrees Minutes Seconds (<+|-> DDD° MM' SSS.ss\")—The coordinate format will be Degrees Minutes Seconds packed into a single value with positive or negative direction component at the beginning (<+|-> DDD.MMSSSss). Degrees Minutes Seconds (<+|-> DDD.MMSSSss)—The coordinate format will be Degrees Decimal Minutes with cardinal direction component at the end (DDD° MM.mmm' <N|S|E|W>). Degrees Decimal Minutes (DDD° MM.mmm' <N|S|E|W>)—The coordinate format will be Degrees Decimal Minutes with cardinal direction component at the beginning (<N|S|E|W> DDD° MM.mmm'). Degrees Decimal Minutes (<N|S|E|W> DDD° MM.mmm')—The coordinate format will be Degrees Decimal Minutes with positive or negative direction component at the beginning (<+|-> DDD° MM.mmm'). Degrees Decimal Minutes (<+|-> DDD° MM.mmm')—The coordinate format will be Global Area Reference System. The Global Area Reference System is based on latitude and longitude, dividing and subdividing the world into cells."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.management.CalculateGeometryFieldsAttributeRule(in_table, out_folder, geometry_attributes, {where_clause}, {length_unit}, {area_unit}, {coordinate_format})

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：in_table、out_folder、geometry_attributes、where_clause、length_unit、area_unit、coordinate_format。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。

## 001407: 格網組件未能正確生成。 · 描述

**id**　cd6cbe68a42840dc2a-1

**english**　001407: Grid component(s) did not generate correctly.

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告
- 1401-1500

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-01401-01425-001407.html#description

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-01401-01425-001407.html

**text**　格網組成部分由于其落在指定 XY 容差之外而未能正確生成。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-01401-01425-001407.html#description

## 001407: 格網組件未能正確生成。 · 解決方案

**id**　cd6cbe68a42840dc2a-2

**english**　001407: Grid component(s) did not generate correctly.

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告
- 1401-1500

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-01401-01425-001407.html#solution

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-01401-01425-001407.html

**text**　在生成格網前提高或降低工具中的 XY 容差值。
XY 容差是地理資料庫圖徵之間的最小容許距離。該距離以格網主座標系中指定的單位表示。處于所設置的 XY 容差范圍內的圖徵將被視為重合的圖徵。XY 容差設定的精度至少達到選定地理資料庫資料集的精度，或將容差設定為零。資料庫的精度取決于 X/Y 域比例的倒數，以及該值到格網主座標系的投影。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-01401-01425-001407.html#solution

## 禁用屬性規則 (資料管理工具) · 用法

**id**　cd6eee886d875ef460-2

**english**　Disable Attribute Rules

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 屬性規則工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/disable-attribute-rules.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/disable-attribute-rules.html

**text**　如果您已將屬性規則應用于資料并且正在加載大量資料，則可以使用此工具來禁用一個或多個規則以避免潛在的性能問題。
可以使用啟用屬性規則工具重新啟用規則（如果適用）。
默認情況下，將啟用所有新的屬性規則（無論是單獨創建的還是導入的）。 此工具允許您在準備好強制使用規則邏輯之前將其禁用。
如果在運行此工具時指定了規則名稱和規則類型，則在執行后，工具將驗證指定規則的類型是否與指定的規則類型相匹配。 如果不匹配，則將不會禁用規則。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/data-management/disable-attribute-rules.html#usage

## 禁用屬性規則 (資料管理工具) · 代碼示例

**id**　cd6eee886d875ef460-18

**english**　Disable Attribute Rules

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 屬性規則工具集

**text**　import arcpy
fc = "C:\\MyProject\\MyDatabase.sde\\pro.USER1.campusData"
desc = arcpy.Describe(fc).attributeRules
for rule in desc:
    if rule.isEnabled == True and rule.type == "esriARTConstraint":
        print("Disabling rule: {}".format(rule.name))
        arcpy.management.DisableAttributeRules(fc, rule.name)

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

## 禁用屬性規則 (資料管理工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 屬性規則工具集

**english**　Disable Attribute Rules

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/disable-attribute-rules.html

**id**　cd6eee886d875ef460-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/disable-attribute-rules.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Disable Attribute Rules，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入表 [in_table]、規則名稱 [names]、類型 （可選） [type]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/data-management/disable-attribute-rules.html#parameters

## 描繪 TIN 資料區 (3D Analyst 工具) · 用法

**id**　cd76dcc1be6015f746-2

**english**　Delineate TIN Data Area

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 3D Analyst 工具箱
- TIN 資料集工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/delineate-tin-data-area.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/delineate-tin-data-area.html

**text**　該工具不會消除任何源資料。 它通過將輸入 TIN 的邊重分類為在插值區域中包含或排除對其進行修改。 如果希望保留要處理的 TIN 的未修改版本，可以考慮在執行該工具前使用 復制 TIN 工具創建重復資料集。
在開始邊長分類之前，將對所有三角形邊進行評估。 這將有效撤消任何現有資料區分類。 如果對一次執行的結果不滿意，則可以再次執行生成的 TIN，無需獲取原始資料。
通過該工具，可從 TIN 的有效資料區中移除 TIN 源測量值中由凹包特征生成的極端邊長。 邊長超過最大邊長的三角形將被掩碼為 NoData 區域。 注: 最大邊長值建議通過被視為有效資料區的區域內 TIN 節點的平均間距進行確定。 提供大于平均間距的值。 需要了解用于創建 TIN 的資料才能成功運行該工具。
方法用于確定要評估的三角形。 注: 如果凹包資料部分歸屬為 TIN 的外端點，使用周邊邊（Python 中的 method = &quot;PERIMETER_ONLY&quot;）。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/3d-analyst/delineate-tin-data-area.html#usage

## 描繪 TIN 資料區 (3D Analyst 工具) · 代碼示例

**id**　cd76dcc1be6015f746-16

**english**　Delineate TIN Data Area

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 3D Analyst 工具箱
- TIN 資料集工具集

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/delineate-tin-data-area.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/delineate-tin-data-area.html

**text**　'''****************************************************************************
Name: Define Data Boundary of LAS File
Description: This script demonstrates how to delineate data boundaries of
             LAS files with irregularly clustered points. It is intended for
             use as a script tool with one input LAS file.
****************************************************************************'''
# Import system modules
import arcpy

# Set local variables
inLas = arcpy.GetParameterAsText(0)  # input LAS file
ptSpacing = arcpy.GetParameterAsText(1)  # LAS point spacing
classCode = arcpy.GetParameterAsText(2)  # List of integers
returnValue = arcpy.GetParameterAsText(3)  # List of strings
outTin = arcpy.GetParameterAsText(4)  # TIN created to delineate data area
outBoundary = arcpy.GetParameterAsText(5)  # Polygon boundary file

try:
    # Run LASToMultipoint
    lasMP = arcpy.CreateUniqueName('lasMultipoint', 'in_memory')
    arcpy.ddd.LASToMultipoint(inLas, LasMP, ptSpacing, class_code,
                             "ANY_RETURNS", "", sr, inFormat, zfactor)
    # Run CreateTin
    arcpy.ddd.CreateTin(outTin, sr, "{0} Shape.Z masspoints"\
                       .format(lasMP), "Delaunay")
    # Run CopyTin
    arcpy.ddd.CopyTin(outTin, "{0}_copy".format(outTin))
    # Run DelineateTinDataArea
    maxEdge = ptSpacing * 4
    arcpy.ddd.DelineateTinDataArea(outTin, maxEdge, "PERIMETER_ONLY")
    # Run TinDomain
    arcpy.ddd.TinDomain(outTin, outBoundary, "POLYGON")

except arcpy.ExecuteError:
    print(arcpy.GetMessages())
except Exception as err:
    print(err)

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/3d-analyst/delineate-tin-data-area.html#derived-output

## 描繪 TIN 資料區 (3D Analyst 工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**

**id**　cd76dcc1be6015f746-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/delineate-tin-data-area.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Delineate TIN Data Area，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入 TIN [in_tin]、最大邊長 [max_edge_length]、方法 （可選） [method]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

需具備 3D Analyst 許可方可使用。
趨勢面法工具可通過全局多項式插值法將由數學函數（多項式）定義的平滑表面與輸入采樣點進行擬合。 趨勢表面會逐漸變化，并捕捉資料中的粗尺度模式。

## 趨勢面法的工作原理 · 概念的背景

**id**　cd8e214b117b631732-2

**english**　How Trend works

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Spatial Analyst 工具箱
- 插值分析工具集
- 插值分析工具集概念

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-trend-works.html#F3B

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-trend-works.html

**text**　從概念上講，趨勢插值就像將一張紙放在凸起點（凸起到值的高度）之間。 下圖展示了在平緩傾斜的山丘上的一組高程采樣點。 這張紙是洋紅色的。
一張平面紙無法準確捕捉包含山谷的景觀。 但是，如果將紙彎曲一次，就會更合適。 向數學公式中添加一項會產生類似結果，即平面彎曲。 平面（紙張沒有彎曲）是一個一階多項式（線性）。 它允許一次彎曲是二階多項式（二次），兩次彎曲是三階多項式（三次），依此類推。 此工具最多允許 12 次彎曲（十二階）。 下圖在概念上演示了擬合到山谷的二階多項式。
紙很少會通過實際測量點，從而使趨勢插值成為不精確的插值器。 一些點將位于紙張上方，而其他點將位于紙張下方。 但是，如果將紙張上方每個點的高度相加，并將紙張下方每個點的高度相加，這兩個總和應該相似。 洋紅色表面是通過使用最小二乘回歸擬合獲得的。 所得到的表面可使凸起值與紙張之間的平方差達到最小化。
均方根 (RMS) 誤差越低，插值表面越接近于輸入點。 最常見的多項式階數是一到三。 趨勢表面插值用于創建平滑表面。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-trend-works.html#F3B

## 趨勢面法的工作原理 · 何時使用趨勢插值法

**id**　cd8e214b117b631732-3

**english**　How Trend works

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Spatial Analyst 工具箱
- 插值分析工具集
- 插值分析工具集概念

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-trend-works.html#D88

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-trend-works.html

**text**　趨勢插值會生成一個平滑表面，表示感興趣區域表面的漸變趨勢。 此種插值法適用于以下幾種情況：
當表面在感興趣區域（例如，工業區的污染）上隨區域逐漸變化時，將表面擬合到采樣點。
檢查或排除長期趨勢或全局趨勢的影響。 此類情況下，采用的方法通常為趨勢面分析。
趨勢插值使用描述物理過程（例如污染和風向）的低階多項式創建逐漸變化的表面。 然而，多項式越復雜，就越難賦予其物理意義。 此外，計算表面極易受到異常值（極高值或極低值）的影響，尤其是位于邊緣處。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-trend-works.html#D88

## 趨勢面法的工作原理 · 線性趨勢

**id**　cd8e214b117b631732-5

**english**　How Trend works

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Spatial Analyst 工具箱
- 插值分析工具集
- 插值分析工具集概念

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-trend-works.html#383

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-trend-works.html

**text**　線性趨勢表面插值法用于創建浮點型網格。 使用多項式回歸，對輸入點進行最小二乘表面擬合。 此方法允許您控制用于擬合表面的多項式的階數。 要理解此方法，請考慮一階多項式。 一階線性趨勢表面插值執行平面與輸入點集的最小二乘法擬合。
趨勢表面插值用于創建平滑表面。 生成的表面很少通過原始資料點，因為它對整個表面執行最佳擬合。 如果所用多項式階數高于一階，插值器所生成網格的最大值和最小值可能會超過輸入圖徵資料輸入文件中的最小值和最大值。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-trend-works.html#383

## 趨勢面法的工作原理 · 邏輯型趨勢

**id**　cd8e214b117b631732-6

**english**　How Trend works

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Spatial Analyst 工具箱
- 插值分析工具集
- 插值分析工具集概念

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-trend-works.html#887

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-trend-works.html

**text**　可生成趨勢面的邏輯型選項適用于預測空間中給定的一組位置 (x,y) 處某種現象存在與否（以概率的形式）。 z 值為已分類隨機變量，只有兩種可能的結果，例如，瀕危物種存在或該物種不存在。 這兩個 z 值可以分別編碼為 1 和 0。 此選項可創建一個連續的概率格網，其像元值介于 1 和 0 之間。
最大似然估計用于計算非線性概率表面模型，而無需首先將模型轉換為線性形式。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-trend-works.html#887

**id**　cd962686c473c3d355-2

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告
- 160101-160200

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/160001-170000/tool-errors-and-warnings-160151-160175-160172.html#solution

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/160001-170000/tool-errors-and-warnings-160151-160175-160172.html

**text**　雖然此錯誤可能會發生，但發生的頻率非常低，以至于我們尚且無法確定典型原因，也因此目前沒有可用的解決方案。
如果您能夠使用反饋表單來提供有關導致此錯誤的所用步驟的詳細信息，那么此舉將有助于我們在未來確定和提供解決方案。
幫助信息包括下列內容：
消息 ID 以及錯誤或警告消息
工具的名稱
用于參數的值
相關資料集詳細信息
如果該工具不再處于打開狀態，則您可以在地理處理歷史記錄中找到這些詳細信息。
我們對您所提供的一切信息深表感激。 感謝您為我們改進錯誤代碼文檔所做出的貢獻。

## 001570：所選分析選項要求至少 <value> 個點位于邊界面區域內。 · 描述

**id**　cda0cc03297798269e-1

**english**　001570: The analysis option you selected requires a minimum of <value> points to be within the bounding polygon areas.

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告
- 1501-1600

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-01551-01575-001570.html#description

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-01551-01575-001570.html

**text**　位于您提供的邊界面區域內的點圖徵不到 30 個。 將僅分析邊界區域內的點。 與許多統計資料一樣，至少需要 30 個圖徵才能生成可靠的結果。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-01551-01575-001570.html#description

## 001570：所選分析選項要求至少 <value> 個點位于邊界面區域內。 · 解決方案

**id**　cda0cc03297798269e-2

**english**　001570: The analysis option you selected requires a minimum of <value> points to be within the bounding polygon areas.

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告
- 1501-1600

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-01551-01575-001570.html#solution

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-01551-01575-001570.html

**text**　遺憾的是，如果點數沒有達到 30 個，則該方法不適用于您的資料。 然而，即使至少擁有 30 個點，該解決方案通常還是提供不同，或許更大的邊界面區域。
另一種方案為提供至少具有 30 個聚合面的圖層，這些面會疊加至少 30 個點。 提供聚合面后，將在每個區域中執行點計數分析。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-01551-01575-001570.html#solution

了解有關使用“區域分組”創建單獨區域的詳細信息

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

## 區域分組 (Spatial Analyst 工具) · 用法

**id**　cda1bbeb3e8d33767c-2

**english**　Region Group

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Spatial Analyst 工具箱
- 概化工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/region-group.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/region-group.html

**text**　通常，掃描的第一個區域接收值 1，第二個區域接收值 2，依此類推，直到所有區域都已賦值。 掃描將按從左至右、從上至下的順序進行。 分配給輸出區域的值取決于掃描過程中系統掃描與區域的相遇時間。
以下兩個參數用于控制如何在區域之間建立連通性。 要使用的鄰居數參數可確定連通性的幾何為正交（四向）還是對角與正交（八向）。 區域分組方法參數可確定評估連通性時要考慮的像元值。
默認情況下，向輸出添加鏈接欄位（Python 中的 ADD_LINK）參數已啟用。 這將在輸出網格的屬性表中創建 LINK 欄位，其保留輸入網格的每個像元的原始區域值。 僅當將區域分組方法（Python 中的 zone_connectivity）參數設置為位于時，此參數才適用。 如果將其設置為交叉，則輸出網格的屬性表將僅包含常見的 Value 和 Count 欄位。
如果可用，LINK 欄位可用于將每個新創建區域的來源追溯回原始輸入區域值以用于其他分析。 例如，上圖所示的輸出網格的屬性表如下：
如果已設置掩膜環境，則可改變輸出網格中的空間配置和區域數。
輸出時，包含排除的值的像元位置會接收零，因此，這些區域便不會與現有 NoData 像元位置相混淆。 由于編號從值 1 開始，因此被排除在重新分組操作之外的像元將被視為背景。 這些背景像元可以相同的方式被重新分類或處理為任何其他值。
有關適用于此工具的地理處理環境的詳細信息，請參閱分析環境和 Spatial Analyst。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/region-group.html#usage

## 區域分組 (Spatial Analyst 工具) · 參數

**id**　cda1bbeb3e8d33767c-5

**english**　Region Group

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Spatial Analyst 工具箱
- 概化工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/region-group.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/region-group.html

**parameter**　number_neighbors

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 要使用的鄰居數 （可選）
- 指定評估定義區域的像元間的連通性時使用的相鄰像元數。 四—評估每個輸入像元中四個最近（正交）相鄰像元的連通性。 只有具有相同值且至少共享一側的像元才會組成單個區域。 如果兩個具有相同值的像元彼此只是對角線連接，則其不會被視為相連接。 這是默認設置。 八—評估每個輸入像元中八個最近相鄰像元（正交和對角線）的連通性。 沿公共邊或角相互連接的具有相同值的像元將構成單個區域。
- String

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/region-group.html#parameters

## 區域分組 (Spatial Analyst 工具) · 參數

**id**　cda1bbeb3e8d33767c-6

**english**　Region Group

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Spatial Analyst 工具箱
- 概化工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/region-group.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/region-group.html

**parameter**　zone_connectivity

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 區域分組方法 （可選）
- 定義在測試連接性時應考慮哪些像元值。 位于—將針對部分同一區域（像元值）的輸入像元評估區域的連通性。 只能對滿足空間連通性要求（由四向或八向要使用的鄰居數參數指定）的同一區域中的像元進行分組。 這是默認設置。 交叉—評估任何值的像元間區域的連通性（不包括由排除值參數排除的區域像元），并遵守由要使用的鄰居數參數指定的空間要求。 輸入中通過 NoData 像元的緩衝區獨立于其他分組的區域分組將彼此獨立地進行處理。
- String

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/region-group.html#parameters

**id**　cda1bbeb3e8d33767c-7

**english**　Region Group

**kind**　參數

- 標注
- 說明

**values**　
- 為輸出資料增加鏈接欄位 （可選）
- 指定將區域分組方法參數設置為位于時是否向輸出表添加鏈接欄位。 如果將該參數設置為交叉，則將忽略此參數。 選中—將 LINK 欄位添加到輸出網格的表中。 根據要使用的鄰居數參數中定義的連通性規則，該欄位將存儲輸出中每個區域的像元所屬區域的值。 這是默認設置。 未選中—將不會添加 LINK 欄位。 輸出網格的屬性表僅包含 Value 和 Count 欄位。
- Boolean

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/region-group.html#parameters

- 從連通性評估中排除該區域所有像元的值。 如果像元位置包含該值，則不管將鄰近像元數指定為多少，都不會評估空間連通性。 具有排除值的像元與 NoData 像元將以相似的方式進行處理，且在運算中不在考量范圍內。 在輸出網格上，包含排除值的輸入像元將接收 0。 排除值類似于背景值的概念。 默認情況下，此參數未定義任何值，這表示在運算中將考慮所有輸入像元。
